Fuzzing (Schemathesis)

Requests generated from docs/api/openapi.json: a 5xx answer or a connection dropped without an answer is a finding. 502 and 503 come from the scratch hub's missing node daemon and git host. Reported here; findings do not fail the pull request yet.

No findings.
